- **e33chat-chat-pipeline**: Use when touching E33Chat message classification, whisper detection, echo suppression, deduplication, quote pairing, player blocking, or sender identification. Covers the three ChatListener channels, the ThreadLocal PENDING_META handoff, the mandatory consumption order in ChatComponentMixin.captureMessage, all TTL/timeout constants, the five independent dedup layers, and the verbatim design invariants that cannot be derived from reading any single file. - **e33chat-tri-platform-parity**: Use when changing any E33Chat logic that must be mirrored across its three platform branches (Forge-1.20.1, Neoforge-1.21.1, Fabric-1.21.1), or when auditing whether a past change was fully propagated. Maps every file/package/mixin-config difference between branches, gives the Forge→Neo→Fabric translation table (packet classes, Yarn mappings, config package layout, suggestion mixins), and records which files are byte-identical vs structurally divergent. Read this BEFORE fixing a bug in shared chat logic — three-way manual writing propagates code faithfully but never surfaces defects. ([NereaFantasia/E33Chat](https://github.com/NereaFantasia/E33Chat/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/e33chat-tri-platform-parity) · ★3)

- **e5-bugfix**: Internal (run by /e5-bugfix). The gated workflow for fixing a bug in the E5 platform - intake, locate at the deployed version, root cause, fix design with blast radius, LLD, then a failing test first. Use whenever the user reports something on the platform that is broken, wrong, stuck or failing. ([kavinraj-e5/e5-platform-spec-driven-harness](https://github.com/kavinraj-e5/e5-platform-spec-driven-harness/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/e5-bugfix))
- **e5-correct**: Internal (invoked by e5-feature at Stage 7). Use when the user says the harness got something about the E5 platform wrong, or when an answer carries risk signals suggesting it may be wrong. Records the correction so the same mistake is not repeated for the next person. Also use when the user asks how to correct or teach the knowledge base. ([kavinraj-e5/e5-platform-spec-driven-harness](https://github.com/kavinraj-e5/e5-platform-spec-driven-harness/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/e5-correct))
- **e5-feature**: THE entry point for any change to the E5 platform. Use whenever the user describes something they want the platform to do, stop doing, or do differently - "I want X to happen when Y", "can we add", "customers need" - even if they never say the word feature. Orchestrates the whole gated workflow and invokes the internal e5 skills itself. Use BEFORE opening any repository.
